## 1 · What was built and why

ZYRA's shared navigation now supports seven visual treatments and five
developer-selectable haptic feel levels. The current default produces one
Subtle selection tick when the finger moves into a different icon. It stays
silent when the active icon is selected again and does not repeat while several
pointer updates remain inside the same icon cell.

The behaviour is part of `FloatingNavBar`, rather than duplicated in Seeker and
Guardian shells, because both account experiences use the same physical
control. This keeps tap, drag-scrub and action-mode feedback consistent. The
current visual/haptic combinator evidence is authoritative in
`ZYRA_STYLE_LAB_COMBINATOR_HANDOVER_2026-08-29_V1.md`.

The Seeker destination order was also aligned across the live shell and
developer controls:

`Browse/Search · Connections · Dashboard · Guardian · Profile`

## 2 · Cross-platform behaviour

Flutter's platform service maps `HapticFeedback.selectionClick()` to:

- iOS 10+: `UISelectionFeedbackGenerator`.
- Android: `HapticFeedbackConstants.CLOCK_TICK`.

No custom vibration duration or strength is requested. The operating system,
device hardware and the person's system haptic settings remain authoritative,
so Android strength can vary by manufacturer and either platform may suppress
feedback when system haptics are disabled.

## 3 · Data and interaction flow

```text
finger touches floating bar
        ↓
bar expands; current cell becomes the comparison point
        ↓
tap releases OR drag crosses into another cell
        ↓
cell differs from last cell? ── no ──> no haptic, no duplicate action
        │ yes
        ↓
HapticFeedback.selectionClick()
        ↓
tab mode: route selection updates immediately while scrubbing
action mode: highlight/tick updates, action commits only on release
```

## 5 · Developer controls; mock versus production

- Preview Control and Action Control use the same destination order as the
  live Seeker navigation.
- Haptics are produced by the live shared widget on real device interactions;
  there is no mock haptic mode, fake persisted state or entitlement bypass.
- Widget tests intercept Flutter's platform channel only inside the test
  process so they can count requested selection feedback deterministically.

## 6 · Limitations and remaining verification

- Automated tests prove which platform feedback request is emitted and that it
  is not duplicated. They cannot measure physical motor strength.
- Founder device observation remains required for the final feel on the target
  iPhone. Android hardware should be observed on at least one representative
  device before release because actuator strength varies.
- If system haptics are disabled, ZYRA deliberately respects that setting.

## 7 · Migrations and persistence

No migration was created or applied. No schema, Supabase policy, stored
preference or account state changed.

## 16. Whole-app One Journey concept prepared on 8 October 2026

### Founder request and authority boundary

The Founder requested a coherent no-swipe comparison across the app, not
another detached Browse mock-up: one Journey per Seeker, unavailable people
removed from search, deliberate requests from either participant, and mutual
acceptance/readiness before beginning together. This is an idea to explore,
not adoption of new live admission, visibility, membership or Journey policy.
AD-AIOS-111 remains the authority overlay. The direct request authorises this
protected local implementation; internal checks are not Founder acceptance.

Home → Discovery direction → **One Journey · Whole-app concept** replaces the
old View & request draft entry. The existing developer admission/account
generation guard remains. The preview covers the current app with a disposable
synthetic shell rather than changing real AppState or sending member actions.
Close, Current ZYRA and the Settings exit return to the exact retained current
app stack; reopening starts fresh. Ordinary Home/Preview/Actions controls are
suppressed only while that exact concept owner holds a denial-only lease.
Revocation, sign-out, account-generation change, disposal and covered routes
refuse stale actions. No persistent mode preference was introduced.

### As-built flow and current-ZYRA reconciliation

Seeker navigation retains the canonical five destinations: Discover,
Connections, My ZYRA, Guardians and Profile. Guardian comparison retains
Dashboard, Network, Seekers and Profile. Local role switching does not switch
the authenticated account. Canonical navigation, brand-gradient colours,
Poppins body text, Syne wordmark and matching actions are reused; screens
scroll when necessary. Incoming-only fade/descent avoids overlapping outgoing
content and respects reduced motion. Local gold/notification comparisons do
not change saved member preferences or restore the Home entries removed in
section 15. Bundled code-native sample illustrations are not verified people.

Discover is photos-first viewing with private Saves and a deliberate Journey
request, not swipe/Pass/Consider. Received and Sent requests remain visible
without consuming a Journey place or being automatically rejected. Acceptance
rechecks both participants and reserves the single place during preparation.
Both are hidden from Discover during preparation, Active and pending Pause.
Each participant separately marks ready, then explicitly begins. Merely
accepting reveals neither private compatibility nor an open conversation.

The shared Journey uses all eight existing stage labels, not a new invented
shortcut: Salam and private compatibility require both participants' choices;
Main Guardian holds apply to both sides; conversation opens only at the final
stage. No compatibility score, answer authority, credit or payment proof is
fabricated. My ZYRA reflects the same local state and connects to the relevant
next action. Sample messages are isolated by Journey ID.

Main/Additional Guardian support and non-family relationships remain distinct.
Additional View and Chat permissions belong to the particular current attempt;
turning View off clears Chat rather than silently restoring it later.
Preparation does not grant current-Journey or Guardian visibility. The
Guardian-role comparison is permitted context, not Seeker browse, private
Saves, pending requests, or authority to edit the Seeker's grants.

Paused and Ended remain separate history sections. Pending Pause still holds
the place; this draft releases it only after simulated mutual settlement.
Resume requires mutual choices, both places free and both Main Guardians;
End is terminal/read-only and does not promise reopening the old attempt.
Explicit scenario controls can show a received request, occupied Journey,
history, Main absence or peer choices. They use fictional records only.

Membership choices reuse current Pricing V6 as a display, including Basic's
£4.99 once, Seeker One's £4.99 first-month comparison then £29.99/month and
Family's £4.99 per person first-month comparison. Standalone Guardian One
retains its current £19.99 ordinary price; no held introductory offer is
invented. This concept's universal one-place constraint does not silently
remove current production One benefits or create purchases/entitlements.

TestFlight/upload or public release occurred. Shared registers were not
edited concurrently. Existing OA-ZYRA-151 owns discovery/policy decisions,
OA-ZYRA-286 owns protected preview and local delivery, and OA-ZYRA-257 owns
physical/design acceptance; no duplicate OA or release closure is asserted.

### Deliberately open decisions and handover limits

Production settlement is still needed for whether Paused consumes the place
or restores discovery, request quotas/expiry/cooldowns/anti-spam, outstanding
requests while occupied, and how One/Family benefits change under one Journey.
This preview releases a mutually settled Pause for comparison and preserves
pending requests; those choices are not adopted production rules. Actual
auth/provider, commerce, server concurrency, real member/photo data, native
accessibility, full release testing and Founder phone/UI acceptance are not
proven by the synthetic checks. Screenshot-protection, translation and other
held release work were not added. Only the Founder may accept this concept or
authorise its conversion into production behaviour.

## §29 Real-member direction recorded; all-toggle subtext cleanup — 2026-10-09

Founder asked to make the new style the default, retain the old presentation as
Option 1 and remove explanatory toggle subheadings across sections. Asked whether
this meant developer-only default sample testing, Founder expressly answered:
“Make the new style the real member flow”. DR-ZYRA-265 records that direction;
OA-ZYRA-151 is now OPEN for its authoritative contract, not an unscoped parked
question. Implementation and activation are distinct from this product direction.

### Real-member review and current stop

The existing concept shell cannot be an ordinary account root: it owns a local
fictional request model, simulated other-user decisions, messages, membership and
Guardian controls. Its account/generation/admission/lease boundary remains intact.
Existing real HomeShell, Browse, Connections and Dashboard controllers remain the
ordinary routes; their account-scoped reads, signed photos and truthful failure
states are the reuse path. Pure presentation widgets can be extracted, not their
sample controller. The Browse renderer's data-image-only wrapper needs a real
image-builder seam; neutral next/previous cannot reuse the current `_advance`
unchanged because it consumes the Basic swipe allowance.

Read-only source facts:

- `zyra_discovery_service.dart:461` writes a `likes` fact. The mutual-interest
  trigger creates Potential matches; this is not explicit recipient acceptance.
- `zyra_journey_service.dart:494` promotes an existing Potential. It is not a
  first-contact request or two-user preparation reservation/readiness contract.
- `requestNewJourney` around1274 concerns existing ended-pair returns; its release
  fuse remains false. The staged September return RPC requires an ended match.
- The September staged paid-only cap gives funded paid Seeker3 / other0. Funded
  Basic1 needs separate commercial reconciliation; no preview selection is proof.
- Browse profile visibility includes shared-attempt and Guardian exemptions.
  Availability must be a separate Browse projection, not global `is_active=false`
  or a blanket profile-read ban that strands current Journey/Guardian participants.
- Real My ZYRA can reuse the one-page presenter and resolver, but its current
  adapter summarizes only the first active Journey and routes request notifications
  to Connections. Incoming/outgoing requests and history need real-row adaptation.

These are checked-in source/staged-contract facts, not measured live deployment
state. New caller-scoped first-contact request/acceptance authority, atomic
two-person places including agreed preparation, Basic1/One3/FamilySeeker3, mutual
readiness and capacity-aware Browse are needed. First-request limits/expiry,
reservation abandonment/expiry and legacy Potential/Paused/pending/Ended mapping
remain decisions; no canonical preparing match status, timeout, silent conversion
or deletion is invented. Existing ritual/chat/compatibility/report/Guardian gates

## §39 Current Browse full photo downward dismissal — 2026-10-10

DR273 adds a viewing gesture to the full photo opened from Current Browse.
`lib/dev/zyra_one_journey_concept_shell.dart` passes `_bound(_back)` through
`_photoSurface` only for a Seeker on Browse, with a single `profile` page,
the Current `portraitCard` design and no confirmation. It removes only that
internal viewing page; the existing Browse index, filter and design remain.
Request confirmations and full profiles from galleries, request lists,
Journey, Guardian or owner preview are not opted in. Existing close/menu
controls and all request/lifecycle authority remain unchanged.

`lib/dev/zyra_one_journey_discovery_presentation.dart` adds optional `onDismiss`
to `ZyraOneJourneyBrowsePresentation`, defaulting to null. Its existing photo
tap detector can opt into a vertical drag. Downward movement translates the
photo; upward movement cannot produce a negative offset. A deliberate pull
of 14% viewport height, or a positive 650px/s fling after 24px displacement,
commits a 200ms ease-out dismissal. A short or cancelled pull returns in 180ms.
Reduced motion/accessibility skips animated release delay. Photo taps still
step through the same supplied photos; no zoom/pager/decision action is added.
The component calls the original caller delegate, never `Navigator.pop`.

An opt-in raw pointer listener tracks only current-render pointer IDs. It
revokes actual pointer cancellation before Flutter's accepted drag can report
it as an end event. Cancelled updates/releases cannot commit; a reduced-motion
commit uses only a same-dispatch microtask boundary, not an animation or timer,
so cancellation also wins when proxied release/cancel callbacks arrive in the
reverse order. Both actual-pointer and direct-ordering cases are tested.

Render, gesture profile, delegate and motion epochs invalidate retained or
in-flight callbacks on parent rebuild/replacement/disposal. Animation completion
cannot borrow a later delegate, and the shell's owner/route/role/revision guard
remains the final authority. Neither gesture nor cancellation writes model,
preferences, requests or member records. Default-null presentations keep their
existing appearance and behavior; Current's bounded card/footer and all ten
gallery choices remain unchanged.

`test/one_journey_photo_dismiss_test.dart` owns 30 pure gesture and delayed-action
checks. Six added shell cases in `test/view_request_draft_widget_test.dart`
exercise actual Current photo entry, same filtered person/deck/style return
from nonzero index with both motion settings, short/upward pulls, confirmation
denial, retained callback denial and unchanged gallery dismissal behavior.
Existing broader photo/filter/lifecycle/owner tests remain in scope.

## §47 Connection recovery and shared ZYRA wordmark — 2026-10-10

The Founder additionally reports a recurring connection recovery screen and
a white ZYRA wordmark instead of the approved gold. The screenshot's ZY-DSHM
is an opaque object-derived reference, not a fixed diagnostic identifier.
It cannot establish the phone's original failure without matching diagnostic
evidence. This finding and the local recovery repairs remain traced to OA151.

Local review identifies a same-account recovery gap: AuthGate records an
Auth-stream error, but previously ignores a later successful same-owner token
refresh. The repair reruns the existing resolver once after fresh
successful recovery, retaining owner/generation/request and preview/admission
guards. It does not treat a recovered event as admission proof or reuse an
old protected authorization snapshot. Timeouts and unspecified ClientException
failures are not proof that the phone is offline. Timeouts and explicit service
failures now use service-unavailable recovery; an unspecified ClientException
does not imply offline, and a generic refresh-token network failure does not
imply an expired session. Genuine transport and explicit invalid-session cases
retain their recovery paths. Connection copy now says “We couldn’t connect to
ZYRA.” AuthGate and four SessionRouter failure logs omit raw exceptions in
favour of error type/opaque reference. This is scoped redaction, not an
app-wide logging audit. The recovery flag is consumed before the resolver;
healthy duplicate events cannot cause a retry loop, and a new owner, disposal
or revoked preview grant cannot inherit a recovery snapshot.

The shared `ZyraText.wordmark` default is aligned with the existing approved
`ZyraColors.splashWordmark` (#E5CE8E), in production and the test-font branch.
This corrects recovery, app-lock and membership headers that relied on its
previous white fallback. Explicit dim footer overrides remain intentional;
ordinary page headings retain their existing foreground colour and typography.
No new colour token or palette change is introduced.

The Supabase skill is used for the local Auth-stream investigation. Official
[Auth event documentation](https://supabase.com/docs/reference/dart/auth-onauthstatechange)
confirms network refresh failures are emitted as stream errors. The changelog
was read; no relevant pinned Flutter API change is selected. Tests use synthetic
sessions and intercepted HTTP only; no live database, provider or member writes.
An additional startup observation is recorded under OA151 for follow-up, not
silently repaired here: the pinned SDK marks initialization before all local
Auth initialization finishes, so a timed-out initialization and repeated Retry
need a separately tested single-flight treatment. Main startup initialization
and admission behavior are unchanged in this bounded repair. It is not proven
to be the cause of this screenshot and no fallback bypass is selected.

Final TEST_FINAL_2 passes 834 checks across 33 scoped regression suites.
ANALYZE_FINAL_3 reports no issues in 20 Dart files; FORMAT_FINAL_2 reports

## §51 Current Browse photo motion and visual feedback — 2026-10-10

The Founder directly requests smoother/slower photo taps, a thicker sent Journey
invitation outline, removal of the dark notch where moving photos meet, and a
slightly larger three-dot button with visible feedback when it changes the bottom
navigation. OA151 owns this scoped refinement; ADMIN remains sole register writer.
Standing HOME delivery applies after checks; physical acceptance remains separate.

Current's adjacent photo taps change from 220 ms ease-out cubic to 420 ms
ease-in-out cubic. The previous curve completed 87.5 percent of its travel halfway
through. Wrapping across three or more photographs updates the index once and
fades the outgoing photograph over 420 ms, never traversing intermediate indices.
Only the photograph moves/fades: the identity, scrim, markers, action/footer
geometry and caller-owned person/eligibility remain. Historical pager timing and
finger-driven paging stay unchanged. Quiet/accessibility settings retain immediate
updates, including revoking an in-flight owned transition.

The private pager receives an immutable photo-source snapshot and independent
motion epoch. It rejects extra taps while sliding/fading, clears the overlay on
genuine swipes and cancels old motion on source/person/count/design changes or
disposal. A same-count source replacement always settles the controller to the
intended selected index, even if its old fractional position rounds to that index;
this prevents an old animation from selecting a replacement photograph later.
The existing route/owner/action guards are unchanged.

Current's individual moving photographs are no longer rounded independently.
The unchanged outer viewport supplies the rounded card corners, so page seams
cannot expose triangular background notches at the top or bottom. Historical
photo/thumbnail rounding stays. The decorative sent-only gold outline is fixed
at 2.5 logical pixels instead of one, retaining the same alpha, fade, no fill,
pointer/semantics exclusion and exact card bounds.

The three-dot glyph changes from 20 to 24px, retaining the existing 48px target.
Opening Browse actions gives the same bottom pill one finite gold edge/soft wash;
quiet settings use a static subtle edge. The cue clears on actual first touch,
preventing a fixed resting edge from conflicting with the real bar's breathing
geometry. Its decoration has no action or accessibility authority. No shared
normal-navbar implementation, action routing or permission guard changes.

## §64 Old/new feature reuse and route-parity review — 2026-10-11

While §63 is checked, the Founder adds an emphatic full old/new app review:
the new methodology must not discard built unchanged features. Two supplied
screenshots compare the retained Find by ZYRA Code page, with camera/manual
entry, against the new unavailable Scan illustration. This request authorizes
the scoped parity review and preparation; it is not an exact live migration,
provider/payment enablement or release-upload GO. All integration findings
below remain actionable under existing OA-ZYRA-151; related legacy Hide is
already OA-ZYRA-217 and staged known-person discovery is OA-ZYRA-295. ADMIN
retains register ownership. No new IDs, closures or live proof are invented.

Root and three read-only reviewers trace Seeker/Guardian navigation, Browser
utilities, account leaves, onboarding/auth, photos, Bio, Journey stages and
archive behaviour in the current local Source. These are source/route findings,
not physical camera, deployed RPC, native accessibility, real-user lifecycle
or release acceptance tests. Existing Source is not rewritten by this review.
The Supabase skill/security guidance informs backend/owner boundaries; no
backend service, auth mutation or database query is executed.

### Root cause and invariant

The earlier work is retained, not deleted. `HomeShell`/`WaliHomeShell` remain
the ordinary member destinations. The Founder's locally admitted request-style
default mounts a separate `ViewRequestDraftScreen`/concept shell; selecting it
does not integrate real-member features. That shell already reuses several
pure visual components, but its data/actions remain fictional or intentionally
held. The internal preview client denies live data reads/writes, and its
denial-only preview lease cannot become member authority. Presenting this
surface as a completed functional replacement would be false. The correction
is to compose existing coordinators/repositories through explicit identity-bound
adapters, not to create another parallel app or remove the protection latch.

Keep one typography/palette/card system, one authenticated startup/onboarding
pipeline and the existing stage/consent/safety authorities. Change only the
approved Browse/request methodology, current activity placement, retired
Save/Pause/restart presentation and necessary archive/target adapters. The
Founder's removed features are not restored merely to achieve old-screen parity.

### Destination-to-reuse map

The table uses Dart file aliases under `SOURCE/lib`: paths beginning with
`screens/`, `services/`, `models/` or `widgets/` are relative to that directory;
bare filenames refer to their unique basename there. Line anchors refer to this
review's pinned source in §63 PRESTATE/current four-delta candidate. The existing implementations
remain available, but existence is not proof that their backend dependencies are
deployed or that the new routes are connected.

### As-built reuse and boundaries

- `widgets/zyra_known_person_code_content.dart` extracts the original manual
  input, Scan code pill, checked-person card and camera capture. The original
  `FindByZyraCodeScreen` mounts that same content/capture and retains its existing
  service/Consider logic in the old flow. Normal fonts, palette and pill styling
  are retained. At >150% text scale, result actions stack and wrap rather than
  truncate. Actual MobileScanner is shared, not a replacement QR illustration.
- `dev/zyra_draft_code_entry.dart` composes that content in the new Scan route.
  Lookup and View profile are required caller-owned callbacks with no service
  fallback. Inline capture keeps the parent's current-route owner and offers
  manual entry on camera/permission failure. Duplicate capture, malformed or
  >200-character codes, stale text/lookup/result epochs, disposed/covered routes,
  backgrounding and observed caller denial/guard exceptions cannot publish an
  action. Pending checks time out after ten seconds. Temporary native permission
  inactivity is not itself permanent admission revocation.
- The protected shell caches stable Scan adapters per UI revision. It resolves
  only canonical `ZYRA-DRAFT-*` codes to the exact existing local profile record,
  rechecks discoverability/hidden/block/occupied eligibility, and opens View
  profile without automatically sending a Journey invitation or Consider.
  Names/city/photos never stand in for a real UUID. A pull returns one parent
  (profile → Scan → Browse). Real permanent-code lookup/request identity remains
  the trusted integration task from §64; this is not a production-code bridge.
- `HelpSupportScreen.preview` reuses the original searchable FAQ renderer while
  freezing caller/content/mode for its mounted lifetime. It cannot fall through
  to live identity, telemetry, safety-case or native email/account tools on

  not reintroduced. The original Help content/account support path is retained;

## 74. Journey icon comparison sizing

### Founder direction and shared sizing rule

The Founder's 2026-10-12 direct request makes the Journey comparison icons
visually comparable to neighbouring navigation icons. It approves a sizing
correction, not a permanent icon choice. Current remains the ordinary default;
the ten alternatives in §73 remain protected, session-local comparisons under
OA-ZYRA-308. The request does not change the navigation methodology or bar.

The old painter used a common 24-unit nominal canvas while each drawing had
different internal padding. Equal widget dimensions therefore produced visibly
unequal artwork. The shared painter now centres each drawing's visible contour
and fits its longest painted axis to a 21-unit extent within that same canvas.
Uniform scaling preserves each symbol's proportions, original coordinates and
draw order. Counter-scaling the outline keeps the shared 1.8-unit line weight;
the original filled Footsteps toes are retained. Tight contour sampling avoids
unused Bézier control-point space shrinking Linked. Fixed artwork and bounds
are cached once per design, not per animation frame or member session.

The caller's 22px navigation box, selected/inactive scale of 1/.78, colours,
highlight, badge shoulder, bar insets, targets, routes and haptics are unchanged.
The actual Actions dropdown thumbnails now use that same 22px navigation
metric instead of 20px. Normal callers still follow the existing authorized
selection. Revocation, account replacement and stale picker callbacks remain
guarded; no member preference is stored and no icon is chosen for the Founder.

## 81. No Git tooling preparation and native build stop

Founder requested the build repair, Option 2 checks and local iPhone delivery;
the later install hold for two other apps was explicitly revoked by
"OK YOUR NEXT - INSTALL APP PLEASE". AD-AIOS-111 and the no-AI-Git rule remain.
Preparation/internal checks do not self-approve execution or release.

Source tooling now pins the real cached Flutter 3.44.2 stable SDK in
tooling/sdk_pins.json: native Dart, Flutter tool snapshot, genuine version JSON,
tool package configuration and upstream xcode_backend.dart. No SDK bytes or
version metadata are forged/changed. no_git_driver.mjs supports format/analyze/
test through the cached native binary and snapshot under no_git.sb. The policy
denies Git-named executables and original SDK shell Flutter/Dart entries.
NO_GIT_CHECK_FINAL records immediate and nested-child exec-denial using only
harmless /usr/bin/false, never an attempted Git command. SDK pins recheck after
supported commands; cache/version/policy drift stops.

The prepared overlay contains four native entry changes preserving original
actions: Runner/production scheme prepare, Runner build and embed_and_thin call
tooling/zyra_xcode_backend.sh. They are not the final shared native configuration.
The exact protected marker selects a separately sandboxed cached-Dart adapter
around the unchanged pinned upstream Context; only shell Flutter re-entry is
redirected to the cached tool snapshot. Wrong markers deny. Its prepared
explicit human-opt-in branch is not used by the final shared native entries.
The driver strips that opt-in; AI never invokes run.sh. After the native stop,
the three shared native files were restored byte-for-byte to PRE, and run.sh's
executable body exactly matches PRE; only its AI/human instruction comment
changes. MANUAL_ROUTE_RESTORED records this comparison without SDK execution.

### 94.1 Sharing and stage boundaries

The retained life-snapshot/Bio concept is a distinct sharing step between
Compatibility Reveal and Guardian Gate, not a ninth canonical server stage.
The protected local shell begins it only after both Compatibility reveals and
both ordinary Continue decisions. It holds the canonical stage at Compatibility
while Bio is open. Both Bio reveal decisions precede the selected details;
both separate Bio Continue decisions atomically finish Bio and advance to Wali.
Ordinary stage advance cannot skip an open Bio step. Legacy canonical callers
retain their eight-stage contract but never acquire Bio permission by skipping.
Detached validated snapshots preserve the seven local disclosure flags.

## 103 Hidden internal controls and same-page close

Founder direct instruction, 17 October 2026 / OA-ZYRA-151/304: remove the
permanent developer rail and use a three-second screen hold to open controls,
with top-left X returning to the exact prior page. The Founder expressly
excludes this feature from the live/customer app. Existing default-OFF internal
build policy, exact protected Founder proof and preview admission remain;
hidden entry is presentation, never authorization. No auth/backend/provider,
schema, dependency, payment, consent, native or signing change is made.

Packet `OA151_HIDDEN_DEV_CONTROLS_2026_10_17.ybJRwl` preserves true 4,788-input
PRE, full public lib/test preimages and five current-document preimages. Eight
runtime paths change: `lib/main.dart`, `lib/dev/dev_signup_process.dart`,
`lib/dev/dev_scenario_panel_v2.dart`, `lib/dev/wali_dev_scenario_panel.dart`,
`lib/dev/zyra_dev_control_system.dart`, `lib/dev/zyra_global_dev_overlay.dart`,
`lib/dev/zyra_one_journey_concept_shell.dart` and the new
`lib/dev/zyra_dev_hold_to_open.dart`. The final public input count is 4,792.
Exact test/driver changes and before/after hashes belong to CHECKED_QUEUED.

All four rail mount points are removed: concept, global Entry, retained Seeker
and retained Guardian. The unused rail class/test contract remains preserved,
not mounted or repurposed as an invisible tap target. Controls are full-screen
in-place UI rather than a new Navigator route; otherwise the existing caller's
current-route authorization would correctly deny its own retained controls.
Global Page options/Preview/Home move inside the page. The concept keeps its
existing categories and separate Developer Home action. X only closes controls.

The concept retains one stable Offstage/TickerMode/ExcludeFocus product subtree
under the overlay. Open/X changes only a controls presentation epoch, not the
actual page stack/tab, UI/data/Circle lease revision, preferences or checkpoint.
Scroll, photo, editor, nested Navigator and unsent Circle state remain owned by
the original page. X and selectors reject prior-open delegates after close/
reopen; page-bound actions deny while covered. The default Back comparison
does not add a second Back button to controls; X is its single visible close.
Holding a formerly unsupported reader/editor opens generic controls, not Back.

The pure recognizer has a three-second deadline and normal gesture arena/slop
behavior. Short, cancelled, moving or multi-finger holds do not open. A
successful hold consumes the press before opening, preventing release from
also tapping the underlying button. Normal child long presses, text selection
and scrolling win first. Admission is checked at start/completion; errors deny.
Owner/callback/page/route replacement, lifecycle suspension, coverage, unmount
and ABA cancel pending holds. No accessibility shortcut bypasses the gesture.

Monotonic route epochs invalidate dispatch synchronously and notify safely
after Navigator build. The root observer is registered only under the existing
internal build flag. Fresh nested onboarding, concept account and Circle
observers share the cancellation stamp without sharing one observer instance
across Navigators. Thus a nested push/pop cannot revive the old hold merely
because the same page/context returns. No routing permission is widened.

The global repaint proxy advances its revision synchronously for every raw
notification, even when safe repaint notification is coalesced until after
Navigator build. Both proxy identity and revision are captured in the hold;
same-frame context A→B→A and observer rebinding cannot revive an old press.
This also fixes a real build-phase notification error when HomeShell publishes
its page context from initState. The public null-observer branch mounts no
hold widget/detector/recognizer, while retaining the existing account/auth/
resume host. No account safety is removed to hide the internal controls.

Independent read-only review identifies offstage keyboard focus and the
deferred-context ABA gap; stable ExcludeFocus and the synchronous proxy stamp
address both without replacing the retained child. Reinspection finds no
remaining concrete route/hold gating defect. Existing positive
test drivers now perform physical hold/X/Home; ordinary page Back remains its
real pull/button. Bare panel tests use an explicit UI-presentation request,
while hosted Entry/Seeker/Guardian cases test the actual three-second gesture.
